### User

_source line 903 · 2026-05-20T06:12:01.363Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested running missing evals for 0.6B experiments on server westd-41312 using only cuda:3 (GPUs 0/1/2 are running rho06 training). The task involves:
   - Checking the registry for DONE/success 0.6B experiments missing downstream and/or OOD evals
   - Running downstream eval (lm_eval with arc_easy, arc_challenge, hellaswag, piqa, winogrande) and OOD eval (perplexity on openwebtext and wikipedia) serially on cuda:3
   - Updating the registry immediately after each eval completes
   - Server: westd-41312, account: root, conda base environment, project dir: /root/provenance_weight_training

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - lm_eval harness (v0.4.11) for downstream benchmarks (arc_easy, arc_challenge, hellaswag, piqa, winogrande)
   - Custom eval_perplexity.py for OOD perplexity evaluation on openwebtext and wikipedia
   - CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3 to restrict GPU usage (maps physical GPU3 to cuda:0 in process)
   - nohup for long-running background processes on remote server
   - MCP tools for SSH access (ssh_read_only for reads, ssh_execute for writes)
   - Registry system for tracking experiment metrics
   - acc_norm metric used for downstream_mean calculation where available
   - AutoDL instances with network unreachable (using cached HF datasets)

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `/root/provenance_weight_training/eval_perplexity.py` — OOD eval script
     - Computes held-out perplexity for multiple checkpoints
     - Args: `--checkpoints` (nargs=+), `--data_path`, `--max_docs` (default 5000), `--max_length` (default 2048), `--output_path`
     - Loads model with float16, uses sliding window with stride=max_length//2
     - Prints progress every 500 docs
     - Outputs JSON with loss, perplexity, num_tokens, elapsed_seconds per checkpoint

   - `/tmp/run_all_ood_evals.sh` — Batch OOD eval script created on server
     - Iterates over 5 checkpoints × 2 OOD datasets (openwebtext, wikipedia)
     - Skips if output file already exists
     - Uses CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=3
     - Checkpoints array:
